  "content": "# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-\n\"\"\"\n客户销售统计模块 - FastAPI 接口实现\n\n提供客户列表查询、按区域统计销售额、单客户订单查询等功能。\n使用内存模拟数据，无需外部数据库依赖。\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom datetime import date, datetime\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Query\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel, Field\n\n# ============================================================\n# 数据模型定义\n# ============================================================\n\n\nclass Customer(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"客户信息模型\"\"\"\n    id: int = Field(..., description=\"客户唯一标识\")\n    name: str = Field(..., \"customer_id\": 6, \"product\": \"防火墙C型\", \"amount\": 28000.00, \"order_date\": \"2025-09-05\"},\n    {\"id\": 112, \"customer_id\": 6, \"product\": \"交换机B型\", \"amount\": 12000.00, \"order_date\": \"2025-09-20\"},\n]\n\n# ============================================================\n# FastAPI 应用初始化\n# ============================================================\n\napp = FastAPI(\n    title=\"客户销售统计系统\",\n    description=\"提供客户管理、区域销售统计、订单查询等接口\",\n    version=\"1.0.0\",\n)\n\n# ============================================================\n# 辅助函数\n# ============================================================\n\n\ndef _parse_date(date_str: Optional[str], field_name: str) -> Optional[date]:\n    \"\"\"将日期字符串解析为 date 对象，格式不正确时抛出 400 异常\"\"\"\n    if date_str is None:\n        return None\n    try:\n        return datetime.strptime(date_str, \"%Y-%m-%d\").date()\n    except ValueError:\n        raise HTTPException(\n            status_code=400,\n            detail=f\"{field_name} 格式错误，要求 YYYY-MM-DD，实际值: {date_str}\",\n        )\n\n\ndef _filter_orders_by_date(\n    orders: list[dict],\n    start_date: Optional[date],\n    end_date: Optional[date],\n) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"根据起止日期过滤订单列表\"\"\"\n    result = orders\n    if start_date is not None:\n        result = [o for o in result if datetime.strptime(o[\"order_date\"], \"%Y-%m-%d\").date() >= start_date]\n    if end_date is not None:\n        result = [o for o in result if datetime.strptime(o[\"order_date\"], \"%Y-%m-%d\").date() <= end_date]\n    return result\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# 接口定义\n# ============================================================\n\n\n@app.get(\"/customers\", response_model=PaginatedCustomers, summary=\"获取客户列表\")\ndef get_customers(\n    region: Optional[str] = Query(None, description=\"按区域筛选：华东/华南/华北/西南\"),\n    skip: int = Query(0, ge=0, description=\"跳过的记录数（分页偏移）\"),\n    limit: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=100, description=\"每页返回数量，1~100\"),\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    获取客户列表，支持按区域筛选和分页。\n\n    - **region**: 可选，按区域过滤客户\n    - **skip**: 分页偏移量，默认 0\n    - **limit**: 每页数量，默认 10，最大 100\n    \"\"\"\n    # 区域校验\n    valid_regions = {\"华东\", \"华南\", \"华北\", \"西南\"}\n    if region is not None and region not in valid_regions:\n        raise HTTPException(\n            status_code=400,\n            detail=f\"无效的区域 '{region}'，可选值: {', '.join(sorted(valid_regions))}\",\n        )\n\n    # 按区域过滤\n    filtered = CUSTOMERS\n    if region is not None:\n        filtered = [c for c in filtered if c[\"region\"] == region]\n\n    total = len(filtered)\n    items = filtered[skip: skip + limit]\n\n    return PaginatedCustomers(\n        total=total,\n        items=[Customer(**c) for c in items],\n    )\n\n\n@app.get(\"/sales/by-region\", response_model=list[RegionSales], summary=\"按区域统计销售额\")\ndef get_sales_by_region(\n    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description=\"起始日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD\"),\n    end_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description=\"结束日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD\"),\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    按区域汇总销售额和订单数，支持按日期范围过滤。\n\n    - **start_date**: 可选，起始日期\n    - **end_date**: 可选，结束日期\n    \"\"\"\n    # 日期参数解析与校验\n    sd = _parse_date(start_date, \"start_date\")\n    ed = _parse_date(end_date, \"end_date\")\n    if sd is not None and ed is not None and sd > ed:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"start_date 不能晚于 end_date\")\n\n    # 过滤订单\n    filtered_orders = _filter_orders_by_date(ORDERS, sd, ed)\n\n    # 构建客户ID -> 区域的映射\n    cid_to_region = {c[\"id\"]: c[\"region\"] for c in CUSTOMERS}\n\n    # 按区域聚合\n    region_stats: dict[str, dict] = {}\n    for order in filtered_orders:\n        reg = cid_to_region.get(order[\"customer_id\"])\n        if reg is None:\n            continue\n        if reg not in region_stats:\n            region_stats[reg] = {\"total_amount\": 0.0, \"order_count\": 0}\n        region_stats[reg][\"total_amount\"] += order[\"amount\"]\n        region_stats[reg][\"order_count\"] += 1\n\n    # 转为响应列表，按区域名称排序\n    return [\n        RegionSales(region=reg, total_amount=vals[\"total_amount\"], order_count=vals[\"order_count\"])\n        for reg, vals in sorted(region_stats.items())\n    ]\n\n\n@app.get(\"/customers/{customer_id}/orders\", response_model=list[Order], summary=\"查询单客户订单\")\ndef get_customer_orders(\n    customer_id: int,\n    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description=\"起始日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD\"),\n    end_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description=\"结束日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD\"),\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    查询指定客户的所有订单，支持按日期范围过滤。\n\n    - **customer_id**: 客户ID（路径参数）\n    - **start_date**: 可选，起始日期\n    - **end_date**: 可选，结束日期\n    \"\"\"\n    # 校验客户是否存在\n    if not any(c[\"id\"] == customer_id for c in CUSTOMERS):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f\"客户ID {customer_id} 不存在\")\n\n    # 日期参数解析与校验\n    sd = _parse_date(start_date, \"start_date\")\n    ed = _parse_date(end_date, \"end_date\")\n    if sd is not None and ed is not None and sd > ed:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"start_date 不能晚于 end_date\")\n\n    # 筛选该客户的订单\n    customer_orders = [o for o in ORDERS if o[\"customer_id\"] == customer_id]\n\n    # 按日期过滤\n    customer_orders = _filter_orders_by_date(customer_orders, sd, ed)\n\n    return [Order(**o) for o in customer_orders]\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# 启动入口\n# ============================================================\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    import uvicorn\n    uvicorn.run(app, host=\"0.0.0.0\", port=8000)\n",

============================================================\n# /customers 客户列表接口测试\n# ============================================================\n\n\nclass TestGetCustomers:\n    \"\"\"客户列表接口测试集\"\"\"\n\n    def test_返回全部客户(self, client):\n        \"\"\"默认请求应返回所有 6 个客户\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers\")\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        data = resp.json()\n        assert data[\"total\"] == 6\n        assert len(data[\"items\"]) == 6\n\n    def test_按区域筛选_华东(self, client):\n        \"\"\"筛选华东区域，应返回 2 个客户\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers\", params={\"region\": \"华东\"})\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        data = resp.json()\n        assert data[\"total\"] == 2\n        assert all(c[\"region\"] == \"华东\" for c in data[\"items\"])\n\n    def test_按区域筛选_华南(self, client):\n        \"\"\"筛选华南区域，应返回 2 个客户\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers\", params={\"region\": \"华南\"})\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        data = resp.json()\n        assert data[\"total\"] == 2\n\n    def test_无效区域返回400(self, client):\n        \"\"\"传入不存在的区域应返回 400\"\"\"\n        resp =   \"\"\"分页参数 skip=2, limit=3 应返回第3~5条\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers\", params={\"skip\": 2, \"limit\": 3})\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        data = resp.json()\n ```json
{
  "content": "# 客户销售统计模块\n\n基于 FastAPI 实现的客户销售统计 RESTful API 服务，提供客户列表查询、按区域统计销售额、单客户订单查询等功能。\n\n## 技术栈\n\n| 组件 | 说明 |\n|------|------|\n| Python 3.10+ | 运行环境 |\n| FastAPI | Web 框架 |\n| Pydantic v2 | 参数校验与数据模型 |\n| pytest + httpx | 单元测试 |\n\n## 快速启动\n\n```bash\n# 安装依赖\npip install fastapi uvicorn httpx pytest\n\n# 启动服务（默认端口 8000）\npython customer_api.py\n\n# 运行测试\npytest test_customer.py -v\n```\n\n启动后访问 http://127.0.0.1:8000/docs 可查看 Swagger 交互式文档。\n\n---\n\n## 接口文档\n\n### 1. 获取客户列表\n\n**GET** `/customers`\n\n返回客户列表，支持按区域筛选和分页。\n\n#### 请求参数（Query）\n\n| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |\n|------|------|------|------|\n| region | string | 否 | 按区域筛选，可选值：`华东` / `华南` / `华北` / `西南` |\n| skip | int | 否 | 分页偏移量，默认 `0`，最小 `0` |\n| limit | int | 否 | 每页数量，默认 `10`，范围 `1~100` |\n\n#### 响应示例\n\n```json\n{\n  \"total\": 6,\n  \"items\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": 1,\n      \"name\": \"张三科技有限公司\",\n      \"region\": \"华东\",\n      \"contact\": \"13800001111\",\n      \"created_at\": \"2025-01-15\"\n    }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n#### 错误响应\n\n| 状态码 | 场景 |\n|--------|------|\n| 400 | 区域值不合法 |\n| 422 | 参数类型错误（如 skip 为负数、limit 超出范围） |\n\n---\n\n### 2. 按区域统计销售额\n\n**GET** `/sales/by-region`\n\n按区域汇总销售额和订单数，支持按日期范围过滤。\n\n#### 请求参数（Query）\n\n| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |\n|------|------|------|------|\n| start_date | string | 否 | 起始日期（含），格式 `YYYY-MM-DD` |\n| end_date | string | 否 | 结束日期（含），格式 `YYYY-MM-DD` |\n\n#### 响应示例\n\n```json\n[\n  {\n    \"region\": \"华东\",\n    \"total_amount\": 120500.00,\n    \"order_count\": 4\n  },\n  {\n    \"region\": \"华南\",\n    \"total_amount\": 90000.00,\n    \"order_count\": 4\n  }\n]\n```\n\n#### 错误响应\n\n| 状态码 | 场景 |\n|--------|------|\n| 400 | 日期格式错误 / start_date 晚于 end_date |\n\n---\n\n### 3. 查询单客户订单\n\n**GET** `/customers/{customer_id}/orders`\n\n查询指定客户的所有订单，支持按日期范围过滤。\n\n#### 路径参数\n\n| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |\n|------|------|------|------|\n| customer_id | int | 是 | 客户唯一标识 |\n\n#### 请求参数（Query）\n\n| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |\n|------|------|------|------|\n| start_date | string | 否 | 起始日期（含），格式 `YYYY-MM-DD` |\n| end_date | string | 否 | 结束日期（含），格式 `YYYY-MM-DD` |\n\n#### 响应示例\n\n```json\n[\n  {\n    \"id\": 101,\n    \"customer_id\": 1,\n    \"product\": \"服务器A型\",\n    \"amount\": 50000.00,\n    \"order_date\": \"2025-02-10\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"id\": 102,\n    \"customer_id\": 1,\n    \"product\": \"交换机B型\",\n    \"amount\": 12000.00,\n    \"order_date\": \"2025-03-15\"\n  }\n]\n```\n\n#### 错误响应\n\n| 状态码 | 场景 |\n|--------|------|\n| 400 | 日期格式错误 / start_date 晚于 end_date |\n| 404 | 客户ID不存在 |\n| 422 | customer_id 类型错误（如传入字符串） |\n\n---\n\n## 模拟数据说明\n\n本模块使用内存模拟数据，包含：\n\n- **6 个客户**：分布在华东(2)、华南(2)、华北(1)、西南(1) 四个区域\n- **12 笔订单**：时间跨度 2025-02 ~ 2025-09，涉及服务器、交换机、防火墙、路由器四类产品的销售\n\n## 项目结构\n\n```\n客户销售统计模块/\n├── customer_api.py    # FastAPI 接口实现\n├── test_customer.py   # 单元测试\n└── README.md          # 接口文档\n```\n",
  "file_path": "E:/abc/AIAgent/QoderCN/客户销售统计模块/README.md"
}

## 三个 API 接口

| 接口 | 方法 | 功能 |
|------|------|------|
| `/customers` | GET | 客户列表，支持区域筛选 + 分页 |
| `/sales/by-region` | GET | 按区域统计销售额，支持日期范围 |
| `/customers/{id}/orders` | GET | 单客户订单查询，支持日期范围 |

\"\"\"\n    获取客户列表，支持按区域筛选和分页。\n\n    - **region**: 可选，按区域过滤客户\n    - **skip**: 分页偏移量，默认 0\n    - **limit**: 每页数量，默认 10，最大 100\n    \"\"\"\n    # 区域校验\n    valid_regions = {\"华东\", \"华南\", \"华北\", )\n\n\n@app.get(\"/sales/by-region\", response_model=list[RegionSales], summary=\"按区域统计销售额\")\ndef get_sales_by_region(\n    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description=\"起始日期（含），格式 可选，结束日期\n    \"\"\"\n    # 日期参数解析与校验\n    sd = _parse_date(start_date, \"start_date\")\n    ed = _parse_date(end_date, \"end_date\")\n    if sd is not None and ed is not None and sd > ed:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"start_date 不能晚于 end_date\")\n\n    # 过滤订单\n    filtered_orders = _filter_orders_by_date(ORDERS, sd, ed)\n\n    # 构建客户ID -> 区域的映射\n    cid_to_region = {c[\"id\"]: c[\"region\"] for c in CUSTOMERS}\n\n    # 按区域聚合\n    region_stats: dict[str, dict] = {}\n    for order in filtered_orders:\n        reg = cid_to_region.get(order[\"customer_id\"])\n        if reg is None:\n            continue\n        if reg not in region_stats:\n            region_stats[reg] = {\"total_amount\": 0.0, \"order_count\": 0}\n        region_stats[reg][\"total_amount\"] += order_count=vals[\"order_count\"])\n        for reg, vals in sorted(region_stats.items())\n    ]\n\n\n@app.get(\"/customers/{customer_id}/orders\", response_model=list[Order], summary=\"查询单客户订单\")\ndef get_customer_orders(\n    customer_id: int,\n    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description=\"起始日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD\"),\n    end_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description=\"结束日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD\"),\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    查询指定客户的所有订单，支持按日期范围过滤。\n\n    - **customer_id**: 客户ID（路径参数）\n    - **start_date**: 可选，起始日期\n    - **end_date**: 可选，结束日期\n    \"\"\"\n    # _parse_date(start_date, \"start_date\")\n    ed = _parse_date(end_date, \"end_date\")\n    if sd is not None and ed is not None and sd > ed:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"start_date 不能晚于 end_date\")\n\n    # 筛选该客户的订单\n    customer_orders = [o for o in ORDERS if o[\"customer_id\"] == customer_id]\n\n    # 按日期过滤\n    customer_orders = _filter_orders_by_date(customer_orders, sd, ed)\n\n    return [Order(**o) for o in customer_orders]\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# 启动入口\n# ============================================================\n# /customers 客户列表接口测试\n# ============================================================\n\n\nclass TestGetCustomers:\n    \"\"\"客户列表接口测试集\"\"\"\n\n    def test_返回全部客户(self, client):\n        \"\"\"默认请求应返回所有 6 个客户\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers\")\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        data = resp.json()\n        assert data[\"total\"] == 6\n        assert len(data[\"items\"]) == 6\n\n    def test_按区域筛选_华东(self, client):\n        \"\"\"筛选华东区域，应返回 2 个客户\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers\", params={\"region\": \"华东\"})\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        data = resp.json()\n        assert data[\"total\"] == 2\n        assert all(c[\"region\"] == \"华东\" for c in data[\"items\"])\n\n    def test_按区域筛选_华南(self, client):\n        \"\"\"筛选华南区域，应返回 2 个客户\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers\", params={\"region\": \"华南\"})\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        data = resp.json()\n        assert data[\"total\"] == 2\n\n    def test_无效区域返回400(self, client):\n        \"\"\"传入不存在的区域应返回 400\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers\", params={\"region\": \"西北\"})\n        assert resp.status_code == 400\n        assert \"无效的区域\" in resp.json()[\"detail\"]\n\n    def test_分页_skip_limit(self, client):\n        \"\"\"分页参数 skip=2, limit=3 应返回第3~5条\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers\", params={\"skip\": 2, \"limit\": 3})\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        data = resp.json()\n        assert data[\"total\"] == 6\n        assert len(data[\"items\"]) == 3\n\n    def test_分页_超出总数(self, client):\n        \"\"\"skip 超出总数时应返回空列表\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers\", data = resp.json()\n        # 应有 4 个区域\n        assert len(data) == 4\n        regions = {item[\"region\"] for item in data}\n        assert regions == {\"华东\", \"华南\", \"华北\", \"西南\"}\n\n    def huadong[\"order_count\"] == 4\n\n    def test_日期范围过滤(self, client):\n        \"\"\"限定 2025-06-01 ~ 2025-06-30，应只包含 6 月的订单\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\n            \"/sales/by-region\",\n       total = sum(item[\"total_amount\"] for item in data)\n        assert total == pytest.approx(20500.0)\n\n    def test_仅起始日期(self, client):\n        \"\"\"只传 start_date 应过滤掉该日期之前的订单\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/sales/by-region\", params={\"start_date\": \"2025-09-01\"})\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        data = resp.json()\n        # 9月订单: 109(华东,50000), 110(华东,8500), client):\n        \"\"\"客户1应有 2 笔订单\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers/1/orders\")\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        data = resp.json()\n        assert len(data) == 2\n        assert all(o[\"customer_id\"] == 1 for o in data)\n\n    def test_订单金额正确(self, client):\n        \"\"\"客户1的订单金额应为 50000 和 12000\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers/1/orders\")\n        data = resp.json()\n        amounts = sorted(o[\"amount\"] for o in data)\n        assert amounts == [12000.0, 50000.0]\n\n    def test_按日期过滤(self, client):\n        \"\"\"限定 2025-03-01 ~ 2025-03-31，客户1应只有 1 笔订单\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\n            \"/customers/1/orders\",\n            params={\"start_date\": \"2025-03-01\", \"end_date\": \"2025-03-31\"},\n        )\n        assert 404\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers/999/orders\")\n        assert resp.status_code == 404\n        assert \"不存在\" in resp.json()[\"detail\"]\n\n    def test_customer_id_为字符串返回422(self, ```json
{
  "content": "# 客户销售统计模块\n\n基于 FastAPI 实现的客户销售统计 RESTful API 服务，提供客户列表查询、按区域统计销售额、单客户订单查询等功能。\n\n## 技术栈\n\n| 组件 | 说明 |\n|------|------|\n| Python 3.10+ | 运行环境 |\n| FastAPI | Web 框架 |\n| Pydantic v2 | 参数校验与数据模型 |\n| pytest + httpx | 单元测试 |\n\n## 快速启动\n\n```bash\n# 安装依赖\npip install fastapi uvicorn httpx pytest\n\n# 启动服务（默认端口 8000）\npython customer_api.py\n\n# 运行测试\npytest test_customer.py -v\n```\n\n启动后访问 http://127.0.0.1:8000/docs 可查看 Swagger 交互式文档。\n\n---\n\n## 接口文档\n\n### 1. 获取客户列表\n\n**GET** `/customers`\n\n返回客户列表，支持按区域筛选和分页。\n\n#### 请求参数（Query）\n\n| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |\n|------|------|------|------|\n| region | string | 否 | 按区域筛选，可选值：`华东` / `华南` / `华北` / `西南` |\n| skip | int | 否 | 分页偏移量，默认 `0`，最小 `0` |\n| limit | int | 否 | 每页数量，默认 `10`，范围 `1~100` |\n\n#### 响应示例\n\n```json\n{\n  \"total\": 6,\n  \"items\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": 1,\n      \"name\": \"张三科技有限公司\",\n      \"region\": \"华东\",\n      \"contact\": \"13800001111\",\n      \"created_at\": \"2025-01-15\"\n    }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n#### 错误响应\n\n| 状态码 | 场景 |\n|--------|------|\n| 400 | 区域值不合法 |\n| 422 | 参数类型错误（如 skip 为负数、limit 超出范围） |\n\n---\n\n### 2. 按区域统计销售额\n\n**GET** `/sales/by-region`\n\n按区域汇总销售额和订单数，支持按日期范围过滤。\n\n#### 请求参数（Query）\n\n| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |\n|------|------|------|------|\n| start_date | string | 否 | 起始日期（含），格式 `YYYY-MM-DD` |\n| end_date | string | 否 | 结束日期（含），格式 `YYYY-MM-DD` |\n\n#### 响应示例\n\n```json\n[\n  {\n    \"region\": \"华东\",\n    \"total_amount\": 120500.00,\n    \"order_count\": 4\n  },\n  {\n    \"region\": \"华南\",\n    \"total_amount\": 90000.00,\n    \"order_count\": 4\n  }\n]\n```\n\n#### 错误响应\n\n| 状态码 | 场景 |\n|--------|------|\n| 400 | 日期格式错误 / start_date 晚于 end_date |\n\n---\n\n### 3. 查询单客户订单\n\n**GET** `/customers/{customer_id}/orders`\n\n查询指定客户的所有订单，支持按日期范围过滤。\n\n#### 路径参数\n\n| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |\n|------|------|------|------|\n| customer_id | int | 是 | 客户唯一标识 |\n\n#### 请求参数（Query）\n\n| 参数 | 类型 | 必填 | 说明 |\n|------|------|------|------|\n| start_date | string | 否 | 起始日期（含），格式 `YYYY-MM-DD` |\n| end_date | string | 否 | 结束日期（含），格式 `YYYY-MM-DD` |\n\n#### 响应示例\n\n```json\n[\n  {\n    \"id\": 101,\n    \"customer_id\": 1,\n    \"product\": \"服务器A型\",\n    \"amount\": 50000.00,\n    \"order_date\": \"2025-02-10\"\n  },\n  {\n    \"id\": 102,\n    \"customer_id\": 1,\n    \"product\": \"交换机B型\",\n    \"amount\": 12000.00,\n    \"order_date\": \"2025-03-15\"\n  }\n]\n```\n\n#### 错误响应\n\n| 状态码 | 场景 |\n|--------|------|\n| 400 | 日期格式错误 / start_date 晚于 end_date |\n| 404 | 客户ID不存在 |\n| 422 | customer_id 类型错误（如传入字符串） |\n\n---\n\n## 模拟数据说明\n\n本模块使用内存模拟数据，包含：\n\n- **6 个客户**：分布在华东(2)、华南(2)、华北(1)、西南(1) 四个区域\n- **12 笔订单**：时间跨度 2025-02 ~ 2025-09，涉及服务器、交换机、防火墙、路由器四类产品的销售\n\n## 项目结构\n\n```\n客户销售统计模块/\n├── customer_api.py    # FastAPI 接口实现\n├── test_customer.py   # 单元测试\n└── README.md          # 接口文档\n```\n",
  "file_path": "E:/abc/AIAgent/QoderCN/客户销售统计模块/README.md"
}
```

**安全专项结论：**
- SQL 注入 — 安全（无数据库操作）
- XSS — 安全（纯 JSON 响应）
- 密钥泄露 — 安全（无硬编码凭证）
- 越权访问 — 有风险（无鉴权机制）
- 拒绝服务 — 有风险（无速率限制）

1	# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
2	"""
3	客户销售统计模块 - FastAPI 接口实现
4	
5	提供客户列表查询、按区域统计销售额、单客户订单查询等功能。
6	使用内存模拟数据，无需外部数据库依赖。
7	"""
8	
9	from datetime import date, datetime
10	from typing import Optional
11	
12	from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Query
13	from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
14	
15	# ============================================================
16	# 数据模型定义
17	# ============================================================
18	
19	
20	class Customer(BaseModel):
21	    """客户信息模型"""
22	    id: int = Field(..., description="客户唯一标识")
23	    name: str = Field(..., description="客户名称")
24	    region: str = Field(..., description="所属区域（华东/华南/华北/西南）")
25	    contact: str = Field(..., description="联系方式")
26	    created_at: str = Field(..., description="创建日期")
27	
28	
29	class Order(BaseModel):
30	    """订单信息模型"""
31	    id: int = Field(..., description="订单唯一标识")
32	    customer_id: int = Field(..., description="关联客户ID")
33	    product: str = Field(..., description="产品名称")
34	    amount: float = Field(..., ge=0, description="订单金额（元）")
35	    order_date: str = Field(..., description="下单日期 YYYY-MM-DD")
36	
37	
38	class RegionSales(BaseModel):
39	    """区域销售额统计结果"""
40	    region: str = Field(..., description="区域名称")
41	    total_amount: float = Field(..., description="该区域总销售额")
42	    order_count: int = Field(..., description="该区域订单总数")
43	
44	
45	class PaginatedCustomers(BaseModel):
46	    """客户列表分页响应"""
47	    total: int = Field(..., description="客户总数")
48	    items: list[Customer] = Field(..., description="当前页客户列表")
49	
50	
51	# ============================================================
52	# 模拟数据
53	# ============================================================
54	
55	CUSTOMERS: list[dict] = [
56	    {"id": 1, "name": "张三科技有限公司", "region": "华东", "contact": "13800001111", "created_at": "2025-01-15"},
57	    {"id": 2, "name": "李氏集团", "region": "华南", "contact": "13900002222", "created_at": "2025-03-20"},
58	    {"id": 3, "name": "王五贸易公司", "region": "华北", "contact": "13700003333", "created_at": "2025-05-08"},
59	    {"id": 4, "name": "赵六电子商务", "region": "西南", "contact": "13600004444", "created_at": "2025-06-12"},
60	    {"id": 5, "name": "钱七网络科技", "region": "华东", "contact": "13500005555", "created_at": "2025-07-01"},
61	    {"id": 6, "name": "孙八信息技术", "region": "华南", "contact": "13400006666", "created_at": "2025-08-18"},
62	]
63	
64	ORDERS: list[dict] = [
65	    {"id": 101, "customer_id": 1, "product": "服务器A型", "amount": 50000.00, "order_date": "2025-02-10"},
66	    {"id": 102, "customer_id": 1, "product": "交换机B型", "amount": 12000.00, "order_date": "2025-03-15"},
67	    {"id": 103, "customer_id": 2, "product": "服务器A型", "amount": 50000.00, "order_date": "2025-04-01"},
68	    {"id": 104, "customer_id": 2, "product": "防火墙C型", "amount": 28000.00, "order_date": "2025-05-20"},
69	    {"id": 105, "customer_id": 3, "product": "路由器D型", "amount": 8500.00, "order_date": "2025-06-15"},
70	    {"id": 106, "customer_id": 3, "product": "服务器A型", "amount": 50000.00, "order_date": "2025-07-22"},
71	    {"id": 107, "customer_id": 4, "product": "交换机B型", "amount": 12000.00, "order_date": "2025-07-05"},
72	    {"id": 108, "customer_id": 4, "product": "防火墙C型", "amount": 28000.00, "order_date": "2025-08-10"},
73	    {"id": 109, "customer_id": 5, "product": "服务器A型", "amount": 50000.00, "order_date": "2025-08-01"},
74	    {"id": 110, "customer_id": 5, "product": "路由器D型", "amount": 8500.00, "order_date": "2025-09-12"},
75	    {"id": 111, "customer_id": 6, "product": "防火墙C型", "amount": 28000.00, "order_date": "2025-09-05"},
76	    {"id": 112, "customer_id": 6, "product": "交换机B型", "amount": 12000.00, "order_date": "2025-09-20"},
77	]

79	# ============================================================
80	# FastAPI 应用初始化
81	# ============================================================
82	
83	app = FastAPI(
84	    title="客户销售统计系统",
85	    description="提供客户管理、区域销售统计、订单查询等接口",
86	    version="1.0.0",
87	)
88	
89	# ============================================================
90	# 辅助函数
91	# ============================================================
92	
93	
94	def _parse_date(date_str: Optional[str], field_name: str) -> Optional[date]:
95	    """将日期字符串解析为 date 对象，格式不正确时抛出 400 异常"""
96	    if date_str is None:
97	        return None
98	    try:
99	        return datetime.strptime(date_str, "%Y-%m-%d").date()
100	    except ValueError:
101	        raise HTTPException(
102	            status_code=400,
103	            detail=f"{field_name} 格式错误，要求 YYYY-MM-DD，实际值: {date_str}",
104	        )
105	
106	
107	def _filter_orders_by_date(
108	    orders: list[dict],
109	    start_date: Optional[date],
110	    end_date: Optional[date],
111	) -> list[dict]:
112	    """根据起止日期过滤订单列表"""
113	    result = orders
114	    if start_date is not None:
115	        result = [o for o in result if datetime.strptime(o["order_date"], "%Y-%m-%d").date() >= start_date]
116	    if end_date is not None:
117	        result = [o for o in result if datetime.strptime(o["order_date"], "%Y-%m-%d").date() <= end_date]
118	    return result
119	
120	
121	# ============================================================
122	# 接口定义
123	# ============================================================
124	
125	
126	@app.get("/customers", response_model=PaginatedCustomers, summary="获取客户列表")
127	def get_customers(
128	    region: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="按区域筛选：华东/华南/华北/西南"),
129	    skip: int = Query(0, ge=0, description="跳过的记录数（分页偏移）"),
130	    limit: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=100, description="每页返回数量，1~100"),
131	):
132	    """
133	    获取客户列表，支持按区域筛选和分页。
134	
135	    - **region**: 可选，按区域过滤客户
136	    - **skip**: 分页偏移量，默认 0
137	    - **limit**: 每页数量，默认 10，最大 100
138	    """
139	    # 区域校验
140	    valid_regions = {"华东", "华南", "华北", "西南"}
141	    if region is not None and region not in valid_regions:
142	        raise HTTPException(
143	            status_code=400,
144	            detail=f"无效的区域 '{region}'，可选值: {', '.join(sorted(valid_regions))}",
145	        )
146	
147	    # 按区域过滤
148	    filtered = CUSTOMERS
149	    if region is not None:
150	        filtered = [c for c in filtered if c["region"] == region]
151	
152	    total = len(filtered)
153	    items = filtered[skip: skip + limit]
154	
155	    return PaginatedCustomers(
156	        total=total,
157	        items=[Customer(**c) for c in items],
158	    )
159	
160	
161	@app.get("/sales/by-region", response_model=list[RegionSales], summary="按区域统计销售额")
162	def get_sales_by_region(
163	    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="起始日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD"),
164	    end_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="结束日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD"),
165	):
166	    """
167	    按区域汇总销售额和订单数，支持按日期范围过滤。
168	
169	    - **start_date**: 可选，起始日期
170	    - **end_date**: 可选，结束日期
171	    """
172	    # 日期参数解析与校验
173	    sd = _parse_date(start_date, "start_date")
174	    ed = _parse_date(end_date, "end_date")
175	    if sd is not None and ed is not None and sd > ed:
176	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="start_date 不能晚于 end_date")
177	
178	    # 过滤订单
179	    filtered_orders = _filter_orders_by_date(ORDERS, sd, ed)
180	
181	    # 构建客户ID -> 区域的映射
182	    cid_to_region = {c["id"]: c["region"] for c in CUSTOMERS}
183	
184	    # 按区域聚合
185	    region_stats: dict[str, dict] = {}
186	    for order in filtered_orders:
187	        reg = cid_to_region.get(order["customer_id"])
188	        if reg is None:
189	            continue
190	        if reg not in region_stats:
191	            region_stats[reg] = {"total_amount": 0.0, "order_count": 0}
192	        region_stats[reg]["total_amount"] += order["amount"]
193	        region_stats[reg]["order_count"] += 1
194	
195	    # 转为响应列表，按区域名称排序
196	    return [
197	        RegionSales(region=reg, total_amount=vals["total_amount"], order_count=vals["order_count"])
198	        for reg, vals in sorted(region_stats.items())
199	    ]
200	
201	
202	@app.get("/customers/{customer_id}/orders", response_model=list[Order], summary="查询单客户订单")
203	def get_customer_orders(
204	    customer_id: int,
205	    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="起始日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD"),
206	    end_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="结束日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD"),
207	):
208	    """
209	    查询指定客户的所有订单，支持按日期范围过滤。
210	
211	    - **customer_id**: 客户ID（路径参数）
212	    - **start_date**: 可选，起始日期
213	    - **end_date**: 可选，结束日期
214	    """
215	    # 校验客户是否存在
216	    if not any(c["id"] == customer_id for c in CUSTOMERS):
217	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"客户ID {customer_id} 不存在")
218	
219	    # 日期参数解析与校验
220	    sd = _parse_date(start_date, "start_date")
221	    ed = _parse_date(end_date, "end_date")
222	    if sd is not None and ed is not None and sd > ed:
223	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="start_date 不能晚于 end_date")
224	
225	    # 筛选该客户的订单
226	    customer_orders = [o for o in ORDERS if o["customer_id"] == customer_id]
227	
228	    # 按日期过滤
229	    customer_orders = _filter_orders_by_date(customer_orders, sd, ed)
230	
231	    return [Order(**o) for o in customer_orders]
232	
233	
234	# ============================================================
235	# 启动入口
236	# ============================================================
237	
238	if __name__ == "__main__":
239	    import uvicorn
240	    uvicorn.run(app, host="0.0.0.0", port=8000)

#### Thinking

Now I have both files read. Let me do a thorough code review covering readability, edge cases, performance, and security. Then I'll generate a markdown document with the review results.

Let me analyze the code:

**customer_api.py issues:**

1. **Security - P1**: `host="0.0.0.0"` in the `if __name__ == "__main__"` block binds to all interfaces. This is a security risk for development - should be `127.0.0.1` by default.

2. **Security - P2**: No authentication/authorization. Anyone can query any customer's orders. In a real system, this would need auth. But since this is a demo/mock, it's more of a note.
   
3. **Performance - P1**: The date filtering function parses date strings repeatedly during comparisons, which becomes inefficient with larger datasets. I should pre-compute parsed dates once to avoid redundant conversions.

4. **Performance - P2**: The region sales endpoint rebuilds the customer ID to region mapping on every request instead of caching it.

5. **Edge case - P1**: Using floating-point arithmetic for monetary amounts introduces precision errors that accumulate across multiple additions. Should switch to Decimal for accurate currency handling.

6. **Readability - P2**: The mock data uses untyped dictionaries, making it harder to understand the expected structure and catch errors early.

7. **Readability - P2**: Date fields are stored as strings rather than proper date types, losing type safety and validation.

8. **Security - P2**: The error message for invalid regions echoes user input directly, though FastAPI's JSON response mitigates XSS risk.

9. **Edge case - P2**: The `_filter_orders_by_date` function has a subtle issue where reassigning the `result` variable causes it to filter the already-filtered result on subsequent operations, which works but is confusing to read.

10. **Edge case - P1**: Date parsing with `strptime` doesn't strictly validate input—something like "2025-1-1" would parse successfully even though it doesn't match the expected format.

11. **Performance - P2**: The customer lookup in `get_customer_orders` does a linear scan through the list, though this is negligible with small datasets.

12. **Readability - P2**: The `valid_regions` set gets recreated on every function call instead of being defined once at the module level.

13. **Edge case - P2**: The `contact` field lacks validation for phone number format, and `created_at` should be a proper date type rather than a string.

14. **Security - P2**: The API is missing CORS configuration, rate limiting, and input length validation on query parameters.

15. **Performance - P2**: The `get_sales_by_region` function rebuilds the `cid_to_region` mapping on every request instead of caching it.

16. **Readability - P2**: The test comment about order amounts doesn't match what the test actually validates.

17. **Edge case - P2**: There's no coverage for concurrent access scenarios, though this is less critical for synchronous FastAPI.

18. **Readability - P2**: The `_parse_date` function could be more flexible by accepting datetime objects or supporting multiple date formats.

19. **Security - P2**: Query parameters like `region` and date strings have no length limits, leaving the API vulnerable to extremely long input attacks.

20. **Edge case - P2**: The `_filter_orders_by_date` function parses dates multiple times per order when both date parameters are provided—once for the start date comparison and again for the end date.

Now I'm compiling these findings into a prioritized markdown report, focusing on the most critical issues around performance, security, and edge cases.

78	
79	# ============================================================
80	# FastAPI 应用初始化
81	# ============================================================
82	
83	app = FastAPI(
84	    title="客户销售统计系统",
85	    description="提供客户管理、区域销售统计、订单查询等接口",
86	    version="1.0.0",
87	)
88	
89	# ============================================================
90	# 辅助函数
91	# ============================================================
92	
93	
94	def _parse_date(date_str: Optional[str], field_name: str) -> Optional[date]:
95	    """将日期字符串解析为 date 对象，格式不正确时抛出 400 异常"""
96	    if date_str is None:
97	        return None
98	    try:
99	        return datetime.strptime(date_str, "%Y-%m-%d").date()
100	    except ValueError:
101	        raise HTTPException(
102	            status_code=400,
103	            detail=f"{field_name} 格式错误，要求 YYYY-MM-DD，实际值: {date_str}",
104	        )
105	
106	
107	def _filter_orders_by_date(
108	    orders: list[dict],
109	    start_date: Optional[date],
110	    end_date: Optional[date],
111	) -> list[dict]:
112	    """根据起止日期过滤订单列表"""
113	    result = orders
114	    if start_date is not None:
115	        result = [o for o in result if datetime.strptime(o["order_date"], "%Y-%m-%d").date() >= start_date]
116	    if end_date is not None:
117	        result = [o for o in result if datetime.strptime(o["order_date"], "%Y-%m-%d").date() <= end_date]
118	    return result
119	
120	
121	# ============================================================
122	# 接口定义
123	# ============================================================
124	
125	
126	@app.get("/customers", response_model=PaginatedCustomers, summary="获取客户列表")
127	def get_customers(
128	    region: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="按区域筛选：华东/华南/华北/西南"),
129	    skip: int = Query(0, ge=0, description="跳过的记录数（分页偏移）"),
130	    limit: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=100, description="每页返回数量，1~100"),
131	):
132	    """
133	    获取客户列表，支持按区域筛选和分页。
134	
135	    - **region**: 可选，按区域过滤客户
136	    - **skip**: 分页偏移量，默认 0
137	    - **limit**: 每页数量，默认 10，最大 100
138	    """
139	    # 区域校验
140	    valid_regions = {"华东", "华南", "华北", "西南"}
141	    if region is not None and region not in valid_regions:
142	        raise HTTPException(
143	            status_code=400,
144	            detail=f"无效的区域 '{region}'，可选值: {', '.join(sorted(valid_regions))}",
145	        )
146	
147	    # 按区域过滤
148	    filtered = CUSTOMERS
149	    if region is not None:
150	        filtered = [c for c in filtered if c["region"] == region]
151	
152	    total = len(filtered)
153	    items = filtered[skip: skip + limit]
154	
155	    return PaginatedCustomers(
156	        total=total,
157	        items=[Customer(**c) for c in items],
158	    )
159	
160	
161	@app.get("/sales/by-region", response_model=list[RegionSales], summary="按区域统计销售额")
162	def get_sales_by_region(
163	    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="起始日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD"),
164	    end_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="结束日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD"),
165	):
166	    """
167	    按区域汇总销售额和订单数，支持按日期范围过滤。
168	
169	    - **start_date**: 可选，起始日期
170	    - **end_date**: 可选，结束日期
171	    """
172	    # 日期参数解析与校验
173	    sd = _parse_date(start_date, "start_date")
174	    ed = _parse_date(end_date, "end_date")
175	    if sd is not None and ed is not None and sd > ed:
176	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="start_date 不能晚于 end_date")
177	
178	    # 过滤订单
179	    filtered_orders = _filter_orders_by_date(ORDERS, sd, ed)
180	
181	    # 构建客户ID -> 区域的映射
182	    cid_to_region = {c["id"]: c["region"] for c in CUSTOMERS}
183	
184	    # 按区域聚合
185	    region_stats: dict[str, dict] = {}
186	    for order in filtered_orders:
187	        reg = cid_to_region.get(order["customer_id"])
188	        if reg is None:
189	            continue
190	        if reg not in region_stats:
191	            region_stats[reg] = {"total_amount": 0.0, "order_count": 0}
192	        region_stats[reg]["total_amount"] += order["amount"]
193	        region_stats[reg]["order_count"] += 1
194	
195	    # 转为响应列表，按区域名称排序
196	    return [
197	        RegionSales(region=reg, total_amount=vals["total_amount"], order_count=vals["order_count"])
198	        for reg, vals in sorted(region_stats.items())
199	    ]
200	
201	
202	@app.get("/customers/{customer_id}/orders", response_model=list[Order], summary="查询单客户订单")
203	def get_customer_orders(
204	    customer_id: int,
205	    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="起始日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD"),
206	    end_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="结束日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD"),
207	):
208	    """
209	    查询指定客户的所有订单，支持按日期范围过滤。
210	
211	    - **customer_id**: 客户ID（路径参数）
212	    - **start_date**: 可选，起始日期
213	    - **end_date**: 可选，结束日期
214	    """
215	    # 校验客户是否存在
216	    if not any(c["id"] == customer_id for c in CUSTOMERS):
217	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"客户ID {customer_id} 不存在")
218	
219	    # 日期参数解析与校验
220	    sd = _parse_date(start_date, "start_date")
221	    ed = _parse_date(end_date, "end_date")
222	    if sd is not None and ed is not None and sd > ed:
223	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="start_date 不能晚于 end_date")
224	
225	    # 筛选该客户的订单
226	    customer_orders = [o for o in ORDERS if o["customer_id"] == customer_id]
227	
228	    # 按日期过滤
229	    customer_orders = _filter_orders_by_date(customer_orders, sd, ed)
230	
231	    return [Order(**o) for o in customer_orders]
232	
233	
234	# ============================================================
235	# 启动入口
236	# ============================================================
237	
238	if __name__ == "__main__":
239	    import uvicorn
240	    uvicorn.run(app, host="0.0.0.0", port=8000)
241

94	def _parse_date(date_str: Optional[str], field_name: str) -> Optional[date]:
95	    """将日期字符串解析为 date 对象，格式不正确时抛出 400 异常"""
96	    if date_str is None:
97	        return None
98	    try:
99	        return datetime.strptime(date_str, "%Y-%m-%d").date()
100	    except ValueError:
101	        raise HTTPException(
102	            status_code=400,
103	            detail=f"{field_name} 格式错误，要求 YYYY-MM-DD，实际值: {date_str}",
104	        )

106	
107	def _filter_orders_by_date(
108	    orders: list[dict],
109	    start_date: Optional[date],
110	    end_date: Optional[date],
111	) -> list[dict]:
112	    """根据起止日期过滤订单列表"""
113	    result = orders
114	    if start_date is not None:
115	        result = [o for o in result if datetime.strptime(o["order_date"], "%Y-%m-%d").date() >= start_date]
116	    if end_date is not None:
117	        result = [o for o in result if datetime.strptime(o["order_date"], "%Y-%m-%d").date() <= end_date]
118	    return result

126	@app.get("/customers", response_model=PaginatedCustomers, summary="获取客户列表")
127	def get_customers(
128	    region: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="按区域筛选：华东/华南/华北/西南"),
129	    skip: int = Query(0, ge=0, description="跳过的记录数（分页偏移）"),
130	    limit: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=100, description="每页返回数量，1~100"),
131	):
132	    """
133	    获取客户列表，支持按区域筛选和分页。
134	
135	    - **region**: 可选，按区域过滤客户
136	    - **skip**: 分页偏移量，默认 0
137	    - **limit**: 每页数量，默认 10，最大 100
138	    """
139	    # 区域校验
140	    valid_regions = {"华东", "华南", "华北", "西南"}
141	    if region is not None and region not in valid_regions:
142	        raise HTTPException(
143	            status_code=400,
144	            detail=f"无效的区域 '{region}'，可选值: {', '.join(sorted(valid_regions))}",
145	        )
146	
147	    # 按区域过滤
148	    filtered = CUSTOMERS
149	    if region is not None:
150	        filtered = [c for c in filtered if c["region"] == region]
151	
152	    total = len(filtered)
153	    items = filtered[skip: skip + limit]
154	
155	    return PaginatedCustomers(
156	        total=total,
157	        items=[Customer(**c) for c in items],
158	    )

162	def get_sales_by_region(
163	    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="起始日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD"),
164	    end_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="结束日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD"),
165	):
166	    """
167	    按区域汇总销售额和订单数，支持按日期范围过滤。
168	
169	    - **start_date**: 可选，起始日期
170	    - **end_date**: 可选，结束日期
171	    """
172	    # 日期参数解析与校验
173	    sd = _parse_date(start_date, "start_date")
174	    ed = _parse_date(end_date, "end_date")
175	    if sd is not None and ed is not None and sd > ed:
176	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="start_date 不能晚于 end_date")
177	
178	    # 过滤订单
179	    filtered_orders = _filter_orders_by_date(ORDERS, sd, ed)
180	
181	    # 构建客户ID -> 区域的映射
182	    cid_to_region = {c["id"]: c["region"] for c in CUSTOMERS}
183	
184	    # 按区域聚合
185	    region_stats: dict[str, dict] = {}
186	    for order in filtered_orders:
187	        reg = cid_to_region.get(order["customer_id"])
188	        if reg is None:
189	            continue
190	        if reg not in region_stats:
191	            region_stats[reg] = {"total_amount": 0.0, "order_count": 0}
192	        region_stats[reg]["total_amount"] += order["amount"]
193	        region_stats[reg]["order_count"] += 1
194	
195	    # 转为响应列表，按区域名称排序
196	    return [
197	        RegionSales(region=reg, total_amount=vals["total_amount"], order_count=vals["order_count"])
198	        for reg, vals in sorted(region_stats.items())
199	    ]
200	
201	
202	@app.get("/customers/{customer_id}/orders", response_model=list[Order], summary="查询单客户订单")
203	def get_customer_orders(
204	    customer_id: int,
205	    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="起始日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD"),
206	    end_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="结束日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD"),
207	):
208	    """
209	    查询指定客户的所有订单，支持按日期范围过滤。
210	
211	    - **customer_id**: 客户ID（路径参数）
212	    - **start_date**: 可选，起始日期
213	    - **end_date**: 可选，结束日期
214	    """
215	    # 校验客户是否存在
216	    if not any(c["id"] == customer_id for c in CUSTOMERS):
217	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"客户ID {customer_id} 不存在")
218	
219	    # 日期参数解析与校验
220	    sd = _parse_date(start_date, "start_date")
221	    ed = _parse_date(end_date, "end_date")
222	    if sd is not None and ed is not None and sd > ed:
223	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="start_date 不能晚于 end_date")
224	
225	    # 筛选该客户的订单
226	    customer_orders = [o for o in ORDERS if o["customer_id"] == customer_id]
227	
228	    # 按日期过滤
229	    customer_orders = _filter_orders_by_date(customer_orders, sd, ed)
230	
231	    return [Order(**o) for o in customer_orders]
232	
233	
234	# ============================================================
235	# 启动入口
236	# ============================================================
237	
238	if __name__ == "__main__":
239	    import uvicorn
240	    uvicorn.run(app, host="0.0.0.0", port=8000)
241

{
  "file_path": "E:/abc/AIAgent/QoderCN/客户销售统计模块/customer_api.py",
  "new_string": "def _filter_orders_by_date(\n    orders: list[dict],\n    start_date: Optional[date],\n    end_date: Optional[date],\n) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"根据起止日期过滤订单列表，每条订单的日期只解析一次\"\"\"\n    if start_date is None and end_date is None:\n        return orders\n    parsed = [\n        (o, date.fromisoformat(o[\"order_date\"]))\n        for o in orders\n    ]\n    result = []\n    for order, d in parsed:\n        if start_date is not None and d < start_date:\n            continue\n        if end_date is not None and d > end_date:\n            continue\n        result.append(order)\n    return result",
  "old_string": "def _filter_orders_by_date(\n    orders: list[dict],\n    start_date: Optional[date],\n    end_date: Optional[date],\n) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"根据起止日期过滤订单列表\"\"\"\n    result = orders\n    if start_date is not None:\n        result = [o for o in result if datetime.strptime(o[\"order_date\"], \"%Y-%m-%d\").date() >= start_date]\n    if end_date is not None:\n        result = [o for o in result if datetime.strptime(o[\"order_date\"], \"%Y-%m-%d\").date() <= end_date]\n    return result"
}

#### Thinking

Now I need to update the test file. The main changes:
1. Add API Key header to all requests via the client fixture
2. Add tests for API Key authentication (missing key → 403, invalid key → 403)
3. Fix the docstring that says "130500" to "120500"
4. The Decimal amounts will be serialized as strings in JSON by default with Pydantic v2. Let me check... Actually, by default Pydantic v2 serializes Decimal to float in JSON. So `huadong["total_amount"]` will be a float like 120500.0. The `pytest.approx` should still work.


5. For the amounts in test_订单金额正确, the comparison `[12000.0, 50000.0]` should still work since Decimal values get converted to floats during JSON serialization.

The simplest approach is to update the client fixture to always include the API Key header, then add separate tests for authentication failures.

\"end_date\": \"2025-01-01\"},\n            headers=_AUTH_HEADERS,\n        )\n        assert resp.status_code == 400\n        assert \"不能晚于\" in resp.json()[\"detail\"]\n\n    def test_无匹配订单返回空列表(self, client):\n        \"\"\"日期范围 outside 所有订单时应返回空列表\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\n            \"/sales/by-region\",\n            params={\"start_date\": \"2026-01-01\", \"end_date\": \"2026-12-31\"},\n            headers=_AUTH_HEADERS,\n        )\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        assert resp.json() == []\n\n\n# TestGetCustomerOrders:\n    \"\"\"单客户订单查询接口测试集\"\"\"\n\n    def test_查询客户1的订单(self, client):\n        \"\"\"客户1应有 2 笔订单\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers/1/orders\", headers=_AUTH_HEADERS)\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        data = resp.json()\n        assert len(data) == 2\n        assert all(o[\"customer_id\"] == 1 for o in data)\n\n    def test_订单金额正确(self, client):\n        \"\"\"客户1的订单金额应为 50000 和 12000\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers/1/orders\", headers=_AUTH_HEADERS)\n        data = resp.json()\n        amounts = sorted(o[\"amount\"] for o in data)\n        assert amounts == [12000.0, 50000.0]\n\n    def test_按日期过滤(self, client):\n        \"\"\"限定 2025-03-01 ~ 2025-03-31，客户1应只有 1 笔订单\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\n            \"/customers/1/orders\",\n            params={\"start_date\": \"2025-03-01\", \"end_date\": \"2025-03-31\"},\n            headers=_AUTH_HEADERS,\n        )\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        data = resp.json()\n        assert len(data) == 1\n        assert data[0][\"id\"] == 102\n\n    def test_不存在的客户返回404(self, client):\n        \"\"\"查询不存在的客户应返回 404\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers/999/orders\", headers=_AUTH_HEADERS)\n        assert resp.status_code == 404\n        assert \"不存在\" in resp.json()[\"detail\"]\n\n    def test_customer_id_为字符串返回422(self, client):\n        \"\"\"customer_id 传入非整数应返回 422\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers/abc/orders\", headers=_AUTH_HEADERS)\n        assert resp.status_code == 422\n\n    def test_日期格式错误返回400(self, client):\n        \"\"\"日期格式不合法应返回 400\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\n            \"/customers/1/orders\",\n            params={\"start_date\": client.get(\n            \"/customers/1/orders\",\n            params={\"start_date\": \"2026-01-01\", \"end_date\": \"2026-12-31\"},\n            headers=_AUTH_HEADERS,\n        )\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        assert resp.json() == []\n\n    def test_客户2订单包含正确产品(self, client):\n        \"\"\"客户2的订单应包含 服务器A型 和 防火墙C型\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers/2/orders\", headers=_AUTH_HEADERS)\n        data = resp.json()\n        products = {o[\"product\"] for o in data}\n        assert products == {\"服务器A型\", \"防火墙C型\"}\n",

#### Thinking

The issue is that Pydantic v2 serializes Decimal as strings in JSON by default. So the amounts come back as strings like "120500.00" instead of floats. I need to update the tests to handle this.

The options are:
1. Configure Pydantic to serialize Decimal as float (use `model_config = {"ser_json_traits": ...}` or a custom serializer)
2. Update tests to compare against string values

Option 1 is better for API usability - clients expect numeric values for amounts, not strings. Let me configure the models to serialize Decimal as numbers.

In Pydantic v2, I can use `field_serializer` or configure the model to use `float` serialization for Decimal fields. The simplest approach is to use a custom serializer or to use `PlainSerializer`.


I'm exploring a few approaches: using `field_serializer` to convert Decimal to float, or wrapping Decimal with `PlainSerializer` to handle the serialization directly in the field definition.

The cleanest solution is to create a reusable type alias that wraps Decimal with a serializer that converts it to float, then apply this to all amount fields across the models.

Pydantic v2 默认将 Decimal 序列化为字符串。需要配置为数值类型输出。

1	# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
2	"""
3	客户销售统计模块 - FastAPI 接口实现
4	
5	提供客户列表查询、按区域统计销售额、单客户订单查询等功能。
6	使用内存模拟数据，无需外部数据库依赖。
7	"""
8	
9	import re
10	from datetime import date, datetime
11	from decimal import Decimal
12	from typing import Annotated, Optional
13	
14	from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Query, Security
15	from fastapi.security import APIKeyHeader
16	from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, PlainSerializer
17	
18	# 金额类型：内部用 Decimal 精确计算，JSON 序列化为 float
19	Money = Annotated[Decimal, PlainSerializer(lambda v: float(v), return_type=float)]
20	
21	# ============================================================
22	# 数据模型定义
23	# ============================================================
24	
25	
26	class Customer(BaseModel):
27	    """客户信息模型"""
28	    id: int = Field(..., description="客户唯一标识")
29	    name: str = Field(..., description="客户名称")
30	    region: str = Field(..., description="所属区域（华东/华南/华北/西南）")
31	    contact: str = Field(..., description="联系方式")
32	    created_at: str = Field(..., description="创建日期")
33	
34	
35	class Order(BaseModel):
36	    """订单信息模型"""
37	    id: int = Field(..., description="订单唯一标识")
38	    customer_id: int = Field(..., description="关联客户ID")
39	    product: str = Field(..., description="产品名称")
40	    amount: Money = Field(..., ge=0, description="订单金额（元）")
41	    order_date: str = Field(..., description="下单日期 YYYY-MM-DD")
42	
43	
44	class RegionSales(BaseModel):
45	    """区域销售额统计结果"""
46	    region: str = Field(..., description="区域名称")
47	    total_amount: Money = Field(..., description="该区域总销售额")
48	    order_count: int = Field(..., description="该区域订单总数")
49	
50	
51	class PaginatedCustomers(BaseModel):
52	    """客户列表分页响应"""
53	    total: int = Field(..., description="客户总数")
54	    items: list[Customer] = Field(..., description="当前页客户列表")

85	# ============================================================
86	# FastAPI 应用初始化
87	# ============================================================
88	
89	app = FastAPI(
90	    title="客户销售统计系统",
91	    description="提供客户管理、区域销售统计、订单查询等接口",
92	    version="1.0.0",
93	)
94	
95	# ============================================================
96	# 模块级常量（避免每次请求重复构建）
97	# ============================================================
98	
99	VALID_REGIONS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"华东", "华南", "华北", "西南"})
100	_CID_TO_REGION: dict[int, str] = {c["id"]: c["region"] for c in CUSTOMERS}
101	_CUSTOMER_IDS: set[int] = {c["id"] for c in CUSTOMERS}
102	_DATE_RE: re.Pattern = re.compile(r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$")
103	
104	# ============================================================
105	# API Key 鉴权
106	# ============================================================
107	
108	_API_KEY_HEADER = APIKeyHeader(name="X-API-Key", auto_error=False)
109	_VALID_API_KEYS: set[str] = {"demo-api-key-2026"}
110	
111	
112	async def _verify_api_key(key: Optional[str] = Security(_API_KEY_HEADER)) -> str:
113	    """校验请求头中的 API Key，无效或缺失时返回 403"""
114	    if key is None or key not in _VALID_API_KEYS:
115	        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="API Key 缺失或无效")
116	    return key
117	
118	# ============================================================
119	# 辅助函数
120	# ============================================================
121	
122	
123	def _parse_date(date_str: Optional[str], field_name: str) -> Optional[date]:
124	    """将日期字符串解析为 date 对象，严格校验 YYYY-MM-DD 格式"""
125	    if date_str is None:
126	        return None
127	    if not _DATE_RE.match(date_str):
128	        raise HTTPException(
129	            status_code=400,
130	            detail=f"{field_name} 格式错误，要求 YYYY-MM-DD，实际值: {date_str}",
131	        )
132	    try:
133	        return date.fromisoformat(date_str)
134	    except ValueError:
135	        raise HTTPException(
136	            status_code=400,
137	            detail=f"{field_name} 日期无效: {date_str}",
138	        )
139	
140	
141	def _filter_orders_by_date(
142	    orders: list[dict],
143	    start_date: Optional[date],
144	    end_date: Optional[date],
145	) -> list[dict]:
146	    """根据起止日期过滤订单列表，每条订单的日期只解析一次"""
147	    if start_date is None and end_date is None:
148	        return orders
149	    parsed = [
150	        (o, date.fromisoformat(o["order_date"]))
151	        for o in orders
152	    ]
153	    result = []
154	    for order, d in parsed:
155	        if start_date is not None and d < start_date:
156	            continue
157	        if end_date is not None and d > end_date:
158	            continue
159	        result.append(order)
160	    return result
161	
162	
163	# ============================================================
164	# 接口定义
165	# ============================================================
166	
167	
168	@app.get("/customers", response_model=PaginatedCustomers, summary="获取客户列表", dependencies=[Security(_verify_api_key)])
169	def get_customers(
170	    region: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="按区域筛选：华东/华南/华北/西南", max_length=10),
171	    skip: int = Query(0, ge=0, description="跳过的记录数（分页偏移）"),
172	    limit: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=100, description="每页返回数量，1~100"),
173	):
174	    """
175	    获取客户列表，支持按区域筛选和分页。
176	
177	    - **region**: 可选，按区域过滤客户
178	    - **skip**: 分页偏移量，默认 0
179	    - **limit**: 每页数量，默认 10，最大 100
180	    """
181	    if region is not None and region not in VALID_REGIONS:
182	        raise HTTPException(
183	            status_code=400,
184	            detail=f"无效的区域 '{region}'，可选值: {', '.join(sorted(VALID_REGIONS))}",
185	        )
186	
187	    # 按区域过滤
188	    filtered = CUSTOMERS
189	    if region is not None:
190	        filtered = [c for c in filtered if c["region"] == region]
191	
192	    total = len(filtered)
193	    items = filtered[skip: skip + limit]
194	
195	    return PaginatedCustomers(
196	        total=total,
197	        items=[Customer(**c) for c in items],
198	    )
199	
200	
201	@app.get("/sales/by-region", response_model=list[RegionSales], summary="按区域统计销售额", dependencies=[Security(_verify_api_key)])
202	def get_sales_by_region(
203	    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="起始日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD", max_length=10),
204	    end_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="结束日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD", max_length=10),
205	):
206	    """
207	    按区域汇总销售额和订单数，支持按日期范围过滤。
208	
209	    - **start_date**: 可选，起始日期
210	    - **end_date**: 可选，结束日期
211	    """
212	    sd = _parse_date(start_date, "start_date")
213	    ed = _parse_date(end_date, "end_date")
214	    if sd is not None and ed is not None and sd > ed:
215	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="start_date 不能晚于 end_date")
216	
217	    filtered_orders = _filter_orders_by_date(ORDERS, sd, ed)
218	
219	    region_stats: dict[str, dict] = {}
220	    for order in filtered_orders:
221	        reg = _CID_TO_REGION.get(order["customer_id"])
222	        if reg is None:
223	            continue
224	        if reg not in region_stats:
225	            region_stats[reg] = {"total_amount": Decimal("0"), "order_count": 0}
226	        region_stats[reg]["total_amount"] += order["amount"]
227	        region_stats[reg]["order_count"] += 1
228	
229	    # 转为响应列表，按区域名称排序
230	    return [
231	        RegionSales(region=reg, total_amount=vals["total_amount"], order_count=vals["order_count"])
232	        for reg, vals in sorted(region_stats.items())
233	    ]
234	
235	
236	@app.get("/customers/{customer_id}/orders", response_model=list[Order], summary="查询单客户订单", dependencies=[Security(_verify_api_key)])
237	def get_customer_orders(
238	    customer_id: int,
239	    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="起始日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD", max_length=10),
240	    end_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="结束日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD", max_length=10),
241	):
242	    """
243	    查询指定客户的所有订单，支持按日期范围过滤。
244	
245	    - **customer_id**: 客户ID（路径参数）
246	    - **start_date**: 可选，起始日期
247	    - **end_date**: 可选，结束日期
248	    """
249	    if customer_id not in _CUSTOMER_IDS:
250	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"客户ID {customer_id} 不存在")
251	
252	    # 日期参数解析与校验
253	    sd = _parse_date(start_date, "start_date")
254	    ed = _parse_date(end_date, "end_date")
255	    if sd is not None and ed is not None and sd > ed:
256	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="start_date 不能晚于 end_date")
257	
258	    # 筛选该客户的订单
259	    customer_orders = [o for o in ORDERS if o["customer_id"] == customer_id]
260	
261	    # 按日期过滤
262	    customer_orders = _filter_orders_by_date(customer_orders, sd, ed)
263	
264	    return [Order(**o) for o in customer_orders]
265	
266	
267	# ============================================================
268	# 启动入口
269	# ============================================================
270	
271	if __name__ == "__main__":
272	    import uvicorn
273	    uvicorn.run(app, host="127.0.0.1", port=8000)

83	app = FastAPI(
84	    title="客户销售统计系统",
85	    description="提供客户管理、区域销售统计、订单查询等接口",
86	    version="1.0.0",
87	)
88	
89	# ============================================================
90	# 辅助函数
91	# ============================================================
92	
93	
94	def _parse_date(date_str: Optional[str], field_name: str) -> Optional[date]:
95	    """将日期字符串解析为 date 对象，格式不正确时抛出 400 异常"""
96	    if date_str is None:
97	        return None
98	    try:
99	        return datetime.strptime(date_str, "%Y-%m-%d").date()
100	    except ValueError:
101	        raise HTTPException(
102	            status_code=400,
103	            detail=f"{field_name} 格式错误，要求 YYYY-MM-DD，实际值: {date_str}",
104	        )
105	
106	
107	def _filter_orders_by_date(
108	    orders: list[dict],
109	    start_date: Optional[date],
110	    end_date: Optional[date],
111	) -> list[dict]:
112	    """根据起止日期过滤订单列表"""
113	    result = orders
114	    if start_date is not None:
115	        result = [o for o in result if datetime.strptime(o["order_date"], "%Y-%m-%d").date() >= start_date]
116	    if end_date is not None:
117	        result = [o for o in result if datetime.strptime(o["order_date"], "%Y-%m-%d").date() <= end_date]
118	    return result
119	
120	
121	# ============================================================
122	# 接口定义
123	# ============================================================
124	
125	
126	@app.get("/customers", response_model=PaginatedCustomers, summary="获取客户列表")
127	def get_customers(
128	    region: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="按区域筛选：华东/华南/华北/西南"),
129	    skip: int = Query(0, ge=0, description="跳过的记录数（分页偏移）"),
130	    limit: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=100, description="每页返回数量，1~100"),
131	):
132	    """
133	    获取客户列表，支持按区域筛选和分页。
134	
135	    - **region**: 可选，按区域过滤客户
136	    - **skip**: 分页偏移量，默认 0
137	    - **limit**: 每页数量，默认 10，最大 100
138	    """
139	    # 区域校验
140	    valid_regions = {"华东", "华南", "华北", "西南"}
141	    if region is not None and region not in valid_regions:
142	        raise HTTPException(
143	            status_code=400,
144	            detail=f"无效的区域 '{region}'，可选值: {', '.join(sorted(valid_regions))}",
145	        )
146	
147	    # 按区域过滤
148	    filtered = CUSTOMERS
149	    if region is not None:
150	        filtered = [c for c in filtered if c["region"] == region]
151	
152	    total = len(filtered)
153	    items = filtered[skip: skip + limit]
154	
155	    return PaginatedCustomers(
156	        total=total,
157	        items=[Customer(**c) for c in items],
158	    )
159	
160	
161	@app.get("/sales/by-region", response_model=list[RegionSales], summary="按区域统计销售额")
162	def get_sales_by_region(
163	    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="起始日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD"),
164	    end_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="结束日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD"),
165	):
166	    """
167	    按区域汇总销售额和订单数，支持按日期范围过滤。
168	
169	    - **start_date**: 可选，起始日期
170	    - **end_date**: 可选，结束日期
171	    """
172	    # 日期参数解析与校验
173	    sd = _parse_date(start_date, "start_date")
174	    ed = _parse_date(end_date, "end_date")
175	    if sd is not None and ed is not None and sd > ed:
176	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="start_date 不能晚于 end_date")
177	
178	    # 过滤订单
179	    filtered_orders = _filter_orders_by_date(ORDERS, sd, ed)
180	
181	    # 构建客户ID -> 区域的映射
182	    cid_to_region = {c["id"]: c["region"] for c in CUSTOMERS}
183	
184	    # 按区域聚合
185	    region_stats: dict[str, dict] = {}
186	    for order in filtered_orders:
187	        reg = cid_to_region.get(order["customer_id"])
188	        if reg is None:
189	            continue
190	        if reg not in region_stats:
191	            region_stats[reg] = {"total_amount": 0.0, "order_count": 0}
192	        region_stats[reg]["total_amount"] += order["amount"]
193	        region_stats[reg]["order_count"] += 1
194	
195	    # 转为响应列表，按区域名称排序
196	    return [
197	        RegionSales(region=reg, total_amount=vals["total_amount"], order_count=vals["order_count"])
198	        for reg, vals in sorted(region_stats.items())
199	    ]
200	
201	
202	@app.get("/customers/{customer_id}/orders", response_model=list[Order], summary="查询单客户订单")
203	def get_customer_orders(
204	    customer_id: int,
205	    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="起始日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD"),
206	    end_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="结束日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD"),
207	):
208	    """
209	    查询指定客户的所有订单，支持按日期范围过滤。
210	
211	    - **customer_id**: 客户ID（路径参数）
212	    - **start_date**: 可选，起始日期
213	    - **end_date**: 可选，结束日期
214	    """
215	    # 校验客户是否存在
216	    if not any(c["id"] == customer_id for c in CUSTOMERS):
217	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"客户ID {customer_id} 不存在")
218	
219	    # 日期参数解析与校验
220	    sd = _parse_date(start_date, "start_date")
221	    ed = _parse_date(end_date, "end_date")
222	    if sd is not None and ed is not None and sd > ed:
223	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="start_date 不能晚于 end_date")
224	
225	    # 筛选该客户的订单
226	    customer_orders = [o for o in ORDERS if o["customer_id"] == customer_id]
227	
228	    # 按日期过滤
229	    customer_orders = _filter_orders_by_date(customer_orders, sd, ed)
230	
231	    return [Order(**o) for o in customer_orders]
232	
233	
234	# ============================================================
235	# 启动入口
236	# ============================================================
237	
238	if __name__ == "__main__":
239	    import uvicorn
240	    uvicorn.run(app, host="0.0.0.0", port=8000)

108	    def test_华东区域金额正确(self, client):

113	        # 客户1: 50000 + 12000 = 62000; 客户5: 50000 + 8500 = 58500; 合计 120500
114	        assert huadong["total_amount"] == pytest.approx(120500.0)
115	        assert huadong["order_count"] == 4
116

122	        )

127	        assert total == pytest.approx(8500.0)

120500\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/sales/by-region\", headers=_AUTH_HEADERS)\n        data = resp.json()\n        huadong = next(item for item in data if item[\"region\"] == \"华东\")\n        # 客户1: 50000 + 12000 = 62000; 客户5: 50000 + 8500 = 58500; 合计 120500\n        assert huadong[\"total_amount\"] == pytest.approx(120500.0)\n        assert huadong[\"order_count\"] == 4\n\n    def test_日期范围过滤(self, client):\n        \"\"\"限定 2025-06-01 ~ 2025-06-30，应只包含 6 月的订单\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\n            \"/sales/by-region\",\n            params={\"start_date\": \"2025-06-01\", \"end_date\": \"2025-06-30\"},\n            headers=_AUTH_HEADERS,\n        )\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        data = resp.json()\n        # 6月订单: 105(华北,8500)；订单107实际为7月，不在范围内\n        total = sum(item[\"total_amount\"] for item in data)\n        assert total == pytest.approx(8500.0)\n\n    def test_仅起始日期(self, client):\n        \"\"\"只传 start_date 应过滤掉该日期之前的订单\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\n            \"/sales/by-region\",\n            params={\"start_date\": \"2025-09-01\"},\n            headers=_AUTH_HEADERS,\n        )\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        data = resp.json()\n        # 9月起订单: 110(华东,8500), 111(华南,28000), 112(华南,12000)；订单109为8月\n        total = sum(item[\"total_amount\"] for item in data)\n        assert total == pytest.approx(48500.0)\n\n    def test_日期格式错误返回400(self, client):\n        \"\"\"日期格式不合法应返回 400\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\n            \"/sales/by-region\",\n            params={\"start_date\": \"2025/01/01\"},\n            headers=_AUTH_HEADERS,\n        )\n        assert resp.status_code == 400\n        assert \"格式错误\" in resp.json()[\"detail\"]\n\n    def test_起始晚于结束返回400(self, client):\n        \"\"\"start_date > end_date 应返回 400\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\n            \"/sales/by-region\",\n            params={\"start_date\": \"2025-12-01\", \"end_date\": \"2025-01-01\"},\n            headers=_AUTH_HEADERS,\n        )\n        assert resp.status_code == 400\n        assert \"不能晚于\" in resp.json()[\"detail\"]\n\n    def test_无匹配订单返回空列表(self, client):\n        \"\"\"日期范围 outside 所有订单时应返回空列表\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\n            \"/sales/by-region\",\n            params={\"start_date\": \"2026-01-01\", \"end_date\": \"2026-12-31\"},\n            headers=_AUTH_HEADERS,\n        )\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        assert resp.json() == []\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# /customers/{customer_id}/orders 单客户订单查询测试\n# ============================================================\n\n\nclass TestGetCustomerOrders:\n    \"\"\"单客户订单查询接口测试集\"\"\"\n\n    def test_查询客户1的订单(self, client):\n        \"\"\"客户1应有 2 笔订单\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers/1/orders\", headers=_AUTH_HEADERS)\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        data = resp.json()\n        assert len(data) == 2\n        assert all(o[\"customer_id\"] == 1 for o in data)\n\n    def test_订单金额正确(self, client):\n        \"\"\"客户1的订单金额应为 50000 和 12000\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers/1/orders\", headers=_AUTH_HEADERS)\n        data = resp.json()\n        amounts = sorted(o[\"amount\"] for o in data)\n        assert amounts == [12000.0, 50000.0]\n\n    def test_按日期过滤(self, client):\n        \"\"\"限定 2025-03-01 ~ 2025-03-31，客户1应只有 1 笔订单\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\n            \"/customers/1/orders\",\n            params={\"start_date\": \"2025-03-01\", \"end_date\": \"2025-03-31\"},\n            headers=_AUTH_HEADERS,\n        )\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        data = resp.json()\n        assert len(data) == 1\n        assert data[0][\"id\"] == 102\n\n    def test_不存在的客户返回404(self, client):\n        \"\"\"查询不存在的客户应返回 404\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers/999/orders\", headers=_AUTH_HEADERS)\n        assert resp.status_code == 404\n        assert \"不存在\" in resp.json()[\"detail\"]\n\n    def test_customer_id_为字符串返回422(self, client):\n        \"\"\"customer_id 传入非整数应返回 422\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers/abc/orders\", headers=_AUTH_HEADERS)\n        assert resp.status_code == 422\n\n    def test_日期格式错误返回400(self, client):\n        \"\"\"日期格式不合法应返回 400\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\n            \"/customers/1/orders\",\n            params={\"start_date\": \"not-a-date\"},\n            headers=_AUTH_HEADERS,\n        )\n        assert resp.status_code == 400\n\n    def test_无匹配日期返回空列表(self, client):\n        \"\"\"日期范围无匹配订单时应返回空列表\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\n            \"/customers/1/orders\",\n            params={\"start_date\": \"2026-01-01\", \"end_date\": \"2026-12-31\"},\n            headers=_AUTH_HEADERS,\n        )\n        assert resp.status_code == 200\n        assert resp.json() == []\n\n    def test_客户2订单包含正确产品(self, client):\n        \"\"\"客户2的订单应包含 服务器A型 和 防火墙C型\"\"\"\n        resp = client.get(\"/customers/2/orders\", 1	# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
2	"""
3	客户销售统计模块 - FastAPI 接口实现
4	
5	提供客户列表查询、按区域统计销售额、单客户订单查询等功能。
6	使用内存模拟数据，无需外部数据库依赖。
7	"""
8	
9	import re
10	from datetime import date, datetime
11	from decimal import Decimal
12	from typing import Annotated, Optional
13	
14	from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Query, Security
15	from fastapi.security import APIKeyHeader
16	from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, PlainSerializer
17	
18	# 金额类型：内部用 Decimal 精确计算，JSON 序列化为 float
19	Money = Annotated[Decimal, PlainSerializer(lambda v: float(v), return_type=float)]
20	
21	# ============================================================
22	# 数据模型定义
23	# ============================================================
24	
25	
26	class Customer(BaseModel):
27	    """客户信息模型"""
28	    id: int = Field(..., description="客户唯一标识")
29	    name: str = Field(..., description="客户名称")
30	    region: str = Field(..., description="所属区域（华东/华南/华北/西南）")
31	    contact: str = Field(..., description="联系方式")
32	    created_at: str = Field(..., description="创建日期")
33	
34	
35	class Order(BaseModel):
36	    """订单信息模型"""
37	    id: int = Field(..., description="订单唯一标识")
38	    customer_id: int = Field(..., description="关联客户ID")
39	    product: str = Field(..., description="产品名称")
40	    amount: Money = Field(..., ge=0, description="订单金额（元）")
41	    order_date: str = Field(..., description="下单日期 YYYY-MM-DD")
42	
43	
44	class RegionSales(BaseModel):
45	    """区域销售额统计结果"""
46	    region: str = Field(..., description="区域名称")
47	    total_amount: Money = Field(..., description="该区域总销售额")
48	    order_count: int = Field(..., description="该区域订单总数")
49	
50	
51	class PaginatedCustomers(BaseModel):
52	    """客户列表分页响应"""
53	    total: int = Field(..., description="客户总数")
54	    items: list[Customer] = Field(..., description="当前页客户列表")
55	
56	
57	# ============================================================
58	# 模拟数据
59	# ============================================================
60

62	    {"id": 1, "name": "张三科技有限公司", "region": "华东", "contact": "13800001111", "created_at": "2025-01-15"},
63	    {"id": 2, "name": "李氏集团", "region": "华南", "contact": "13900002222", "created_at": "2025-03-20"},
64	    {"id": 3, "name": "王五贸易公司", "region": "华北", "contact": "13700003333", "created_at": "2025-05-08"},
65	    {"id": 4, "name": "赵六电子商务", "region": "西南", "contact": "13600004444", "created_at": "2025-06-12"},
66	    {"id": 5, "name": "钱七网络科技", "region": "华东", "contact": "13500005555", "created_at": "2025-07-01"},
67	    {"id": 6, "name": "孙八信息技术", "region": "华南", "contact": "13400006666", "created_at": "2025-08-18"},
68	]
69	
70	ORDERS: list[dict] = [
71	    {"id": 101, "customer_id": 1, "product": "服务器A型", "amount": Decimal("50000.00"), "order_date": "2025-02-10"},
72	    {"id": 102, "customer_id": 1, "product": "交换机B型", "amount": Decimal("12000.00"), "order_date": "2025-03-15"},
73	    {"id": 103, "customer_id": 2, "product": "服务器A型", "amount": Decimal("50000.00"), "order_date": "2025-04-01"},
74	    {"id": 104, "customer_id": 2, "product": "防火墙C型", "amount": Decimal("28000.00"), "order_date": "2025-05-20"},
75	    {"id": 105, "customer_id": 3, "product": "路由器D型", "amount": Decimal("8500.00"), "order_date": "2025-06-15"},
76	    {"id": 106, "customer_id": 3, "product": "服务器A型", "amount": Decimal("50000.00"), "order_date": "2025-07-22"},
77	    {"id": 107, "customer_id": 4, "product": "交换机B型", "amount": Decimal("12000.00"), "order_date": "2025-07-05"},
78	    {"id": 108, "customer_id": 4, "product": "防火墙C型", "amount": Decimal("28000.00"), "order_date": "2025-08-10"},

80	    {"id": 110, "customer_id": 5, "product": "路由器D型", "amount": Decimal("8500.00"), "order_date": "2025-09-12"},
81	    {"id": 111, "customer_id": 6, "product": "防火墙C型", "amount": Decimal("28000.00"), "order_date": "2025-09-05"},

84	
85	# ============================================================
86	# FastAPI 应用初始化
87	# ============================================================
88	
89	app = FastAPI(
90	    title="客户销售统计系统",
91	    description="提供客户管理、区域销售统计、订单查询等接口",
92	    version="1.0.0",
93	)
94	
95	# ============================================================
96	# 模块级常量（避免每次请求重复构建）
97	# ============================================================
98	
99	VALID_REGIONS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"华东", "华南", "华北", "西南"})
100	_CID_TO_REGION: dict[int, str] = {c["id"]: c["region"] for c in CUSTOMERS}
101	_CUSTOMER_IDS: set[int] = {c["id"] for c in CUSTOMERS}
102	_DATE_RE: re.Pattern = re.compile(r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$")
103	
104	# ============================================================
105	# API Key 鉴权
106	# ============================================================
107	
108	_API_KEY_HEADER = APIKeyHeader(name="X-API-Key", auto_error=False)
109	_VALID_API_KEYS: set[str] = {"demo-api-key-2026"}
110	
111	
112	async def _verify_api_key(key: Optional[str] = Security(_API_KEY_HEADER)) -> str:
113	    """校验请求头中的 API Key，无效或缺失时返回 403"""
114	    if key is None or key not in _VALID_API_KEYS:
115	        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="API Key 缺失或无效")
116	    return key
117	
118	# ============================================================
119	# 辅助函数
120	# ============================================================
121	
122	
123	def _parse_date(date_str: Optional[str], field_name: str) -> Optional[date]:
124	    """将日期字符串解析为 date 对象，严格校验 YYYY-MM-DD 格式"""
125	    if date_str is None:
126	        return None
127	    if not _DATE_RE.match(date_str):
128	        raise HTTPException(
129	            status_code=400,
130	            detail=f"{field_name} 格式错误，要求 YYYY-MM-DD，实际值: {date_str}",
131	        )
132	    try:
133	        return date.fromisoformat(date_str)
134	    except ValueError:
135	        raise HTTPException(
136	            status_code=400,
137	            detail=f"{field_name} 日期无效: {date_str}",
138	        )
139	
140	
141	def _filter_orders_by_date(
142	    orders: list[dict],
143	    start_date: Optional[date],
144	    end_date: Optional[date],
145	) -> list[dict]:
146	    """根据起止日期过滤订单列表，每条订单的日期只解析一次"""
147	    if start_date is None and end_date is None:
148	        return orders
149	    parsed = [
150	        (o, date.fromisoformat(o["order_date"]))
151	        for o in orders
152	    ]
153	    result = []
154	    for order, d in parsed:
155	        if start_date is not None and d < start_date:
156	            continue
157	        if end_date is not None and d > end_date:
158	            continue
159	        result.append(order)
160	    return result
161	
162	
163	# ============================================================
164	# 接口定义
165	# ============================================================
166	
167	
168	@app.get("/customers", response_model=PaginatedCustomers, summary="获取客户列表", dependencies=[Security(_verify_api_key)])
169	def get_customers(
170	    region: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="按区域筛选：华东/华南/华北/西南", max_length=10),
171	    skip: int = Query(0, ge=0, description="跳过的记录数（分页偏移）"),
172	    limit: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=100, description="每页返回数量，1~100"),
173	):
174	    """
175	    获取客户列表，支持按区域筛选和分页。
176	
177	    - **region**: 可选，按区域过滤客户
178	    - **skip**: 分页偏移量，默认 0
179	    - **limit**: 每页数量，默认 10，最大 100
180	    """
181	    if region is not None and region not in VALID_REGIONS:
182	        raise HTTPException(
183	            status_code=400,
184	            detail=f"无效的区域 '{region}'，可选值: {', '.join(sorted(VALID_REGIONS))}",
185	        )
186	
187	    # 按区域过滤
188	    filtered = CUSTOMERS
189	    if region is not None:
190	        filtered = [c for c in filtered if c["region"] == region]
191	
192	    total = len(filtered)
193	    items = filtered[skip: skip + limit]
194	
195	    return PaginatedCustomers(
196	        total=total,
197	        items=[Customer(**c) for c in items],
198	    )
199	
200	
201	@app.get("/sales/by-region", response_model=list[RegionSales], summary="按区域统计销售额", dependencies=[Security(_verify_api_key)])
202	def get_sales_by_region(
203	    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="起始日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD", max_length=10),
204	    end_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="结束日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD", max_length=10),
205	):
206	    """
207	    按区域汇总销售额和订单数，支持按日期范围过滤。
208	
209	    - **start_date**: 可选，起始日期
210	    - **end_date**: 可选，结束日期
211	    """
212	    sd = _parse_date(start_date, "start_date")
213	    ed = _parse_date(end_date, "end_date")
214	    if sd is not None and ed is not None and sd > ed:
215	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="start_date 不能晚于 end_date")
216	
217	    filtered_orders = _filter_orders_by_date(ORDERS, sd, ed)
218	
219	    region_stats: dict[str, dict] = {}
220	    for order in filtered_orders:
221	        reg = _CID_TO_REGION.get(order["customer_id"])
222	        if reg is None:
223	            continue
224	        if reg not in region_stats:
225	            region_stats[reg] = {"total_amount": Decimal("0"), "order_count": 0}
226	        region_stats[reg]["total_amount"] += order["amount"]
227	        region_stats[reg]["order_count"] += 1
228	
229	    # 转为响应列表，按区域名称排序
230	    return [
231	        RegionSales(region=reg, total_amount=vals["total_amount"], order_count=vals["order_count"])
232	        for reg, vals in sorted(region_stats.items())
233	    ]
234	
235	
236	@app.get("/customers/{customer_id}/orders", response_model=list[Order], summary="查询单客户订单", dependencies=[Security(_verify_api_key)])
237	def get_customer_orders(
238	    customer_id: int,
239	    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="起始日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD", max_length=10),
240	    end_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="结束日期（含），格式 YYYY-MM-DD", max_length=10),
241	):
242	    """
243	    查询指定客户的所有订单，支持按日期范围过滤。
244	
245	    - **customer_id**: 客户ID（路径参数）
246	    - **start_date**: 可选，起始日期
247	    - **end_date**: 可选，结束日期
248	    """
249	    if customer_id not in _CUSTOMER_IDS:
250	        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"客户ID {customer_id} 不存在")
251	
252	    # 日期参数解析与校验
253	    sd = _parse_date(start_date, "start_date")
254	    ed = _parse_date(end_date, "end_date")
255	    if sd is not None and ed is not None and sd > ed:
256	        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="start_date 不能晚于 end_date")
257	
258	    # 筛选该客户的订单
259	    customer_orders = [o for o in ORDERS if o["customer_id"] == customer_id]
260	
261	    # 按日期过滤
262	    customer_orders = _filter_orders_by_date(customer_orders, sd, ed)
263	
264	    return [Order(**o) for o in customer_orders]
265	
266	
267	# ============================================================
268	# 启动入口
269	# ============================================================
270	
271	if __name__ == "__main__":
272	    import uvicorn
273	    uvicorn.run(app, host="127.0.0.1", port=8000)
274
